# TASK-20260724-022 — ledger transition-completeness (frozen build spec)

Card: `TASK-20260724-022` / Linear META-31. Board revision at freeze: **138**.
Baseline: `main` @ `07c5095`, node suite **143/143 green** (`node --test scripts/workplan.test.mjs`).
Writable slice: `scripts/workplan.mjs`, `scripts/workplan.test.mjs`. Nothing else.

## 1. The defect

Deferred codex P2 from the TASK-20260724-019 panel
(`.review-store/t019-codex-review-b141afd.txt:31`), verbatim:

> | P2 | scripts/workplan.mjs:1340; scripts/workplan.mjs:1867 | Append-side transitions do
> not validate the existing ledger or status-to-tail continuity, so a stripped tail is
> permanently re-anchored. | Remove `ready→claimed` from a claimed card and run `start`; the
> new `claimed→in_progress` receipt anchors to `backlog→ready`, and all later chain
> validation passes despite the missing transition. |

Root cause: hash-chaining proves the **integrity of records that are present**. It cannot
prove **completeness**. Stripping the tail leaves a structurally valid, shorter chain; the
next legitimate append re-anchors `prevEntryHash` across the gap and every later validation
passes. `validateReceiptChain`'s own docstring disclaims this
(`scripts/workplan.mjs:1340-1343`).

The narrow precedent already in tree is the verifying-only positional rule
(`scripts/workplan.mjs:2347-2360`). This card generalizes that idea to every transition.

## 2. The invariant

At every append, the ledger tail must be the record that *ended* at the status this
transition is *leaving from*.

**Enforce: `tail.to === from`** — where `from` is the argument already passed to
`appendReceipt`, NOT `card.status`.

### Why `from`, not `card.status` (non-negotiable)

`card.status` is mutated **before** `appendReceipt` at 5 of 9 call sites and **after** at the
other 4. Verified:

| status already NEW at append | status still OLD at append |
|---|---|
| `claim` 1456→1459 · `claim-next` 1553→1556 · `accept` 2445→2463 · `block` 2649→2651 · `ready` 2875→2881 | `start` 1888→1896 · `submit` 1975→1984 · `integrate` 2092→2106 · `resume` 2761→2771 |

Any implementation reading `card.status` inside `appendReceipt` is wrong at half the call
sites. The `from` argument is the pre-transition status at all 9 sites, including `block`
(which passes `card.blockedFrom`, assigned from `card.status` before the overwrite,
`scripts/workplan.mjs:2646-2653`).

## 3. Placement

Inside **`appendReceipt`** (`scripts/workplan.mjs:1304-1331`), before the record is built.

It is the single true choke point: all 9 append sites across 8 mutating commands funnel
through it, and `card.receipts` is already in memory (no new I/O).

**Placement is load-bearing for test compatibility, not just convenience.** The three commands
that already carry receipts-relevant checks run them *before* reaching `appendReceipt`:

- `accept` — `validateIntegrationReceipt` (2416) + `validateReceiptChain` (2417) precede 2463
- `block` — `validateReceiptChain` (2635) precedes 2651
- `resume` — `latestReceipt.to !== "blocked"` guard (2738) + `validateBlockedReceipt` (2740)
  + `validateReceiptChain` (2748) all precede 2761

For the other 6 commands (`ready`, `claim`, `claim-next`, `start`, `submit`, `integrate`) there
are **no** pre-existing receipts-relevant checks, so the new check is the *first* such check at
those sites — there is nothing there for it to preempt. Net: the new check can never displace an
existing error message at any of the 9 sites.

**Do NOT hoist this as a blanket pre-check at command entry.** Doing so breaks at minimum these
three assertions (line numbers are the `assert` lines themselves):
- `scripts/workplan.test.mjs:8001` — expects `/integrationReceipt binding record missing in ledger/`
- `scripts/workplan.test.mjs:8789` — expects `/ledger tail is not the verifying record|integrationReceipt binding hash mismatch/`
- `scripts/workplan.test.mjs:6786` — expects `/is not in blocked status/`

All three construct a tail/status mismatch deliberately and assert on the *existing*
narrower message.

## 4. Legacy tolerance — the exact rule

Tolerate **absence**, never **mismatch**.

1. `card.receipts` absent or empty → **allow** (genesis; also the seeded bootstrap card
   `TASK-20260714-001`, which is `done` with zero receipts on the live board).
2. Tail present → **enforce `tail.to === from` regardless of whether the tail is chained**
   (i.e. regardless of the `entryHash` key). Legacy records are still records; a legacy
   ledger is exactly where this attack is cheapest.
3. On violation, `fail()` with a distinct, greppable message. Proposed:
   `ledger tail ${tail.to} does not match transition from ${from}`.

### Evidence this rule does not brick anything

Live board audit, all 18 cards: **16 cards have a ledger tail, and `tail.to === status` on all
16. The remaining 2 (`TASK-20260714-001`, seeded `done`; `TASK-20260724-022`, `backlog`) carry
no `receipts` key at all and satisfy rule 4.1 vacuously — there is no tail to compare. 0 cards
carry any chained record.** Both hand-authored legacy fixtures are internally consistent —
`scripts/workplan.test.mjs:8123-8132` (tail `ready`, status `ready`) and
`legacyInProgressRoot` `:8317-8342` (tail `in_progress`, status `in_progress`).

Rejected alternative: "enforce only when the tail is chained." Strictly weaker — it would
leave every legacy board (i.e. *the entire live board today*) undefended, which is the
population the attack actually targets.

## 5. Out of scope — do not re-litigate

- **Declared residual**: wholesale removal of all chain keys / rewrite-with-recompute
  (`scripts/workplan.mjs:1368-1376`), pinned by `scripts/workplan.test.mjs:8877`. That test
  strips *fields*, never a *record* — tail still matches status — so it must keep passing
  untouched.
- **codex P1-A mixed-era caveat** — accepted as documented in `.agents/t019-definition.json:13`.
- **The gateway.** The card trace names it, but scout verification found **zero coupling**:
  `development/remote_workplan/gateway.py` never references `workplan.mjs`,
  `.workplan/state.json`, or `card.receipts`; `grep -n "gateway" scripts/workplan.mjs`
  returns nothing. It is a code-disjoint reimplementation over its own SQLite ledger. Treat
  the trace's mention as precautionary. Do not edit it.

## 6. Required tests (each must cite the behavior it pins)

1. **The codex repro, exactly**: legitimately reach `claimed`, strip the `ready→claimed`
   record, run `start` → must fail with the new message. This is the card's reason to exist.
2. Same strip shape on a **fully legacy** (un-chained) ledger → must fail.
3. Genesis: card with no `receipts` key → `ready` succeeds (regression on
   `scripts/workplan.test.mjs:8195`).
4. Empty `receipts` array + non-backlog status → allow (rule 4.1), pinned explicitly since no
   existing test covers it.
5. Happy path per transition: `ready`/`claim`/`start`/`submit`/`integrate`/`block`/`resume`
   still append normally with a consistent tail.
6. `block` uses `blockedFrom` as `from` — a card blocked from `review` must pass the check.
7. `resume` lands in `in_progress` from `blocked` — tail `to:"blocked"` must satisfy `from`.
8. Byte-preservation on rejection: state.json unchanged after a rejected transition
   (mirror the existing `preserves bytes` pattern).

 function canonicalizeRecordForHash(record) {
   const copy = { ...record };
   delete copy.entryHash;
   return JSON.stringify(deepSortKeys(copy));
 }
 
 function computeRecordEntryHash(record) {
   return `sha256:${createHash("sha256")
     .update(canonicalizeRecordForHash(record))
     .digest("hex")}`;
 }

 }
 
 // Validates the per-card ledger's hash chain. A record is "chained" when it
 // carries an entryHash; the predecessor it points at must recompute to that
 // hash, and the chain must be contiguous -- once any record is chained, every
 // later record must be chained too. A fully legacy ledger (zero chained
 // records, or no receipts array at all) is allowed: this validator is meant
 // to be run by accept/block/resume (alongside the other pre-mutation guards),
 // not by validateState, because foreign/legacy boards must remain loadable
 // and projectable. Append-side commands (ready/claim/start/submit/integrate)
 // do not pre-validate: a tampered chained record fails entryHash recompute
 // at the next accept/block/resume regardless of later appends re-anchoring
 // prevEntryHash on top of it.
 //
 // Chained-vs-legacy is decided by the presence of the `entryHash` KEY, not
 // its value: a record with the key set to null is still a chained record
 // that failed to compute and must be rejected (not silently demoted to
 // legacy, which would demote the entire trailing ledger to legacy and
 // silently disable the binding check). The same is true for `prevEntryHash`
 // on a chained record: only the first record is allowed to carry
 // prevEntryHash:null (genesis).
 //
 // Uniform prevRecord rule: for every chained record,
 //   receipt.prevEntryHash === (prevRecord === null
 //                                 ? null
 //                                 : computeRecordEntryHash(prevRecord))
 // This single rule covers three migration cases:
 //   - genesis (no predecessor): expected = null
 //   - legacy-anchor (predecessor is a legacy 7-field record): expected is
 //     the canonicalized hash of that legacy record as stored
 //   - middle-strip (predecessor is any chained record): expected equals
 //     that record's entryHash (since computeRecordEntryHash strips it
 //     before canonicalizing)
 // A tampered middle-strip leaves the gap visible: the successor's stored
 // prevEntryHash points to the deleted record's hash, but the recomputed
 // predecessor is the one immediately before it in the surviving array.
 //
 // Residual (out of scope): wholesale removal of the chain fields (or
 // rewrite-with-recompute). A tampered card that deletes entryHash /
 // prevEntryHash keys from every receipt and rewrites the rest still
 // passes this validator (the ledger reads as fully legacy), and the
 // validateIntegrationReceiptBinding check is disabled too: with zero
 // chained records, it early-returns. Only the pre-existing
 // reviewFreeze correlation checks (and the revision bound) remain in
 // play for the integrationReceipt. Closing that residual requires
 // keyless anchoring (e.g. a per-board hash) and is explicitly deferred.
 function validateReceiptChain(card) {

 test("wholesale removal of every chain field pins the declared residual (tampered integrationReceipt is accepted)", async (context) => {
   const { root, parent } = await repository(
     state([
       card({
         id: "TASK-20260711-001",
         title: "Wholesale chain-field removal residual",
         status: "backlog",
         owner: "",
         paths: ["src/wholesaleremoval"],
       }),
     ]),
   );
   context.after(() => rm(parent, { recursive: true, force: true }));
 
   await seedCommit(root);
   await ensureMainBranch(root);
 
   const { actor } = await reachVerifying(root, "TASK-20260711-001");
 
   // RESIDUAL DOCUMENTATION TEST. Per the validator's declared residual,
   // "wholesale removal of the chain fields (or rewrite-with-recompute)" is
   // out of scope. This test pins that residual: a tampered card that
   // deletes entryHash and prevEntryHash from every receipt, and rewrites
   // card.integrationReceipt to a doctored copy that still passes every
   // other validateIntegrationReceipt check, IS accepted by the current
   // validator. Closing this residual requires a keyless anchor (e.g. a
   // per-board hash) and is explicitly deferred. A future change that
   // closes the residual MUST flip this test intentionally.
   const tampered = JSON.parse(
     await readFile(join(root, ".workplan", "state.json"), "utf8"),
   );
   const tamperedCard = tampered.cards[0];
   for (const receipt of tamperedCard.receipts) {
     delete receipt.entryHash;
     delete receipt.prevEntryHash;
   }
   // Tamper the integrationReceipt's `at` timestamp to a different valid ISO
   // value. The original integrationCommit is preserved (it exists in git)
   // and every other field is left intact, so the only thing this attack
   // changes is the binding hash. With chain fields stripped, the binding
   // check is bypassed and accept succeeds -- pinning the declared residual.
   const tamperedAt = new Date(
     new Date(tamperedCard.integrationReceipt.at).getTime() + 60_000,
   ).toISOString();
   tamperedCard.integrationReceipt = {
     ...tamperedCard.integrationReceipt,
     at: tamperedAt,
   };
   await writeFile(
     join(root, ".workplan", "state.json"),
     `${JSON.stringify(tampered, null, 2)}\n`,
   );
 
   const cardBefore = tampered.cards[0];
   const acceptancePath = join(parent, "acceptance.json");
   await writeAcceptance(acceptancePath, cardBefore, actor);
 
   const result = await run([
     "accept",
     "TASK-20260711-001",
     "--control-root",
     root,
     "--caller-worktree",
     root,
     "--expected-revision",
     "6",
     "--actor",
     actor,
     "--acceptance-json",
     acceptancePath,
   ]);
   assert.equal(result.code, 0, result.stderr);
 
   const finalState = JSON.parse(
     await readFile(join(root, ".workplan", "state.json"), "utf8"),
   );
   const finalCard = finalState.cards[0];
   assert.equal(finalCard.status, "done");
   assert.equal(finalCard.integrationReceipt.at, tamperedAt);
 
   await assert.rejects(stat(join(root, ".workplan", "lock")), {
     code: "ENOENT",
   });
 });

+test("ready allows genesis when the receipts key is absent", async (context) => {
+  // Pins absence tolerance for genesis cards: no receipts key is not a tail
+  // mismatch, and ready creates the first backlog-to-ready record.
+  const { root, parent } = await repository(
+    state([card({ paths: ["src/genesis-absence"] })]),
+  );

+  assert.deepEqual(
+    finalState.cards[0].receipts.map(({ from, to }) => ({ from, to })),

+test("start allows an empty receipts array on a non-backlog card", async (context) => {
+  // Pins the other absence shape explicitly: an empty ledger is allowed even
+  // when the card is claimed, and start creates a new genesis receipt.

+    state([card({ paths: ["src/empty-non-backlog"] })]),

+test("resume accepts a blocked ledger tail and lands in in_progress", async (context) => {
+  // Pins resume's source and destination: tail.to blocked satisfies from
+  // blocked, and the resulting receipt lands in in_progress.

   638	    for (const path of card.paths) {
   639	      if (typeof path !== "string") {
   640	        fail("card.paths entries must be strings");
   641	      }
   642	      validateOwnedPath(path);
   643	    }
   644	    validateCardPaths(card);
   645	    if (seenIds.has(card.id)) {
   646	      fail(`duplicate card id ${card.id}`);
   647	    }
   648	    seenIds.add(card.id);
   649	  }
   650	  validatePathExclusivity(state);
   651	}
   652	
   653	function escapeCell(value) {
   654	  return String(value ?? "").replace(/\|/g, "\\|").replace(/\r?\n/g, " ");
   655	}
   656	
   657	function renderPaths(paths) {
   658	  if (paths.length === 0) return "";
   659	  return paths.map((path) => `\`${escapeCell(path)}\``).join("; ");
   660	}
   661	
   662	function renderWorkplan(state) {
   663	  const lines = [
   664	    "<!-- Generated by workplan sync. Do not edit manually. -->",
   665	    "",
   666	  ];
   667	  if (state.bootstrapException) {
   668	    lines.push(`> Bootstrap exception: ${escapeCell(state.bootstrapException)}`);
   669	    lines.push("");
   670	  }
   671	  lines.push(`Revision: ${state.revision}`);
   672	  lines.push("");
   673	  lines.push("| ID | Title | Status | Owner | Paths |");
   674	  lines.push("|---|---|---|---|---|");
   675	
   676	  const sorted = [...state.cards].sort((a, b) => {
   677	    const orderDiff = STATUS_ORDER[a.status] - STATUS_ORDER[b.status];
   678	    if (orderDiff !== 0) return orderDiff;
   679	    return a.id.localeCompare(b.id);
   680	  });
   681	
   682	  for (const card of sorted) {
   683	    lines.push(
   684	      `| ${escapeCell(card.id)} | ${escapeCell(card.title)} | ${escapeCell(
   685	        card.status,
   686	      )} | ${escapeCell(card.owner)} | ${renderPaths(card.paths)} |`,
   687	    );
   688	  }
   689	
   690	  return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
   691	}
   692	
   693	const ADD_LOCK_OWNER = "workplan:add";
   694	const READY_LOCK_OWNER = "workplan:ready";
   695	const CLAIM_LOCK_OWNER = "workplan:claim";
   696	const TRANSITION_LOCK_OWNER = "workplan:transition";
   697	const KNOWN_LOCK_OWNERS = [
   698	  ADD_LOCK_OWNER,
   699	  READY_LOCK_OWNER,
   700	  CLAIM_LOCK_OWNER,
   701	  TRANSITION_LOCK_OWNER,
   702	];
   703	const LOCK_FILE = "lock";

   706	function makeLockMetadata(owner) {
   707	  return {
   708	    schemaVersion: 1,
   709	    host: hostname(),
   710	    pid: process.pid,
   711	    owner,
   712	  };
   713	}
   714	
   715	function makeTempName(targetName) {
   716	  return `.tmp-${targetName}.${process.pid}.${randomBytes(8).toString("hex")}`;
   717	}
   718	
   719	async function writeAtomicFile(dir, targetName, metadata) {
   720	  const tempPath = join(dir, makeTempName(targetName));
   721	  const targetPath = join(dir, targetName);
   722	  const handle = await open(tempPath, "w", 0o600);
   723	  await handle.writeFile(JSON.stringify(metadata));
   724	  await handle.sync();
   725	  await handle.close();
   726	  try {
   727	    await link(tempPath, targetPath);
   728	  } finally {
   729	    await unlink(tempPath).catch(() => {});
   730	  }
   731	  return targetPath;
   732	}
   733	
   734	async function readLockMetadata(lockPath) {
   735	  let content;
   736	  try {
   737	    content = await readFile(lockPath, "utf8");
   738	  } catch (error) {
   739	    if (error.code === "ENOENT") {
   740	      return { ok: false, reason: "missing" };
   741	    }
   742	    throw error;
   743	  }
   744	  if (content.trim() === "") {
   745	    return { ok: false, reason: "empty" };
   746	  }
   747	  try {
   748	    return { ok: true, metadata: JSON.parse(content) };
   749	  } catch {
   750	    return { ok: false, reason: "malformed" };
   751	  }
   752	}
   753	
   754	function isLiveProcess(pid) {
   755	  try {
   756	    process.kill(pid, 0);
   757	    return true;
   758	  } catch (error) {
   759	    if (error.code === "EPERM") return true;
   760	    if (error.code === "ESRCH") return false;
   930	}
   931	
   932	async function sync(controlRoot) {
   933	  if (!isAbsolute(controlRoot)) {
   934	    fail("--control-root must be an absolute path");
   935	  }
   936	  const statePath = join(controlRoot, ".workplan", "state.json");
   937	  const raw = await readFile(statePath, "utf8");
   938	  const state = JSON.parse(raw);
   939	  validateState(state);
   940	  const output = renderWorkplan(state);
   941	  const workplanPath = join(controlRoot, "WORKPLAN.md");
   942	  const tempPath = join(controlRoot, `WORKPLAN.md.tmp-${process.pid}`);
   943	  await writeFile(tempPath, output);
   944	  await rename(tempPath, workplanPath);
   945	  return { revision: state.revision, synchronized: true };
   946	}

   948	async function add(options) {
   949	  const {
   950	    controlRoot,
   951	    callerWorktree,
   952	    expectedRevision,
   953	    id,
   954	    outcome,
   955	    paths,
   956	    priority,
   957	    trace,
   958	    dependencies,
   959	    evidence,
   960	    next,
   961	  } = options;
   962	
   963	  if (!isAbsolute(controlRoot)) {
   964	    fail("--control-root must be an absolute path");
   965	  }
   966	  if (callerWorktree === undefined) {
   967	    fail("--caller-worktree is required");
   968	  }
   969	  if (!isAbsolute(callerWorktree)) {
   970	    fail("--caller-worktree must be an absolute path");
   971	  }
   972	
   973	  for (const path of paths) {
   974	    validateOwnedPath(path);
   975	  }
   976	
   977	  await assertSameRepository(controlRoot, callerWorktree);
   978	
   979	  const acquired = await acquireAddLock(controlRoot);
   980	  try {
   981	    const statePath = join(controlRoot, ".workplan", "state.json");
   982	    const raw = await readFile(statePath, "utf8");
   983	    const state = JSON.parse(raw);
   984	    validateState(state);
   985	
   986	    if (state.revision !== expectedRevision) {
   987	      fail(
   988	        `expected revision ${expectedRevision} but found ${state.revision}`,
   989	      );
   990	    }
   991	    if (state.cards.some((card) => card.id === id)) {
   992	      fail(`duplicate id ${id}`);
   993	    }
   994	
   995	    const newCard = {
   996	      id,
   997	      title: outcome,
   998	      status: "backlog",
   999	      owner: "workplan:add",
  1000	      paths,
  1001	    };
  1002	    if (priority !== undefined) newCard.priority = priority;
  1003	    if (trace !== undefined) newCard.trace = trace;
  1004	    if (dependencies !== undefined) newCard.dependencies = dependencies;
  1005	    if (evidence !== undefined) newCard.evidence = evidence;
  1006	    if (next !== undefined) newCard.next = next;
  1007	
  1008	    validateCardPaths(newCard);
  1009	    for (const existing of state.cards) {
  1010	      if (!isPathReserving(existing)) continue;
  1011	      for (const newPath of newCard.paths) {
  1012	        for (const existingPath of existing.paths) {
  1013	          if (pathsOverlap(newPath, existingPath)) {
  1014	            fail(
  1015	              `path ${newPath} overlaps with path ${existingPath} on card ${existing.id}`,
  1016	            );
  1017	          }
  1018	        }
  1019	      }
  1020	    }
  1021	
  1022	    state.cards.push(newCard);
  1023	    state.revision += 1;
  1024	
  1025	    const workplanPath = join(controlRoot, "WORKPLAN.md");
  1360	// This single rule covers three migration cases:
  1361	//   - genesis (no predecessor): expected = null
  1362	//   - legacy-anchor (predecessor is a legacy 7-field record): expected is
  1363	//     the canonicalized hash of that legacy record as stored
  1364	//   - middle-strip (predecessor is any chained record): expected equals
  1365	//     that record's entryHash (since computeRecordEntryHash strips it
  1366	//     before canonicalizing)
  1367	// A tampered middle-strip leaves the gap visible: the successor's stored
  1368	// prevEntryHash points to the deleted record's hash, but the recomputed
  1369	// predecessor is the one immediately before it in the surviving array.
  1370	//
  1371	// Residual (out of scope): wholesale removal of the chain fields (or
  1372	// rewrite-with-recompute). A tampered card that deletes entryHash /
  1373	// prevEntryHash keys from every receipt and rewrites the rest still
  1374	// passes this validator (the ledger reads as fully legacy), and the
  1375	// validateIntegrationReceiptBinding check is disabled too: with zero
  1376	// chained records, it early-returns. Only the pre-existing
  1377	// reviewFreeze correlation checks (and the revision bound) remain in
  1378	// play for the integrationReceipt. Closing that residual requires
  1379	// keyless anchoring (e.g. a per-board hash) and is explicitly deferred.
  1380	function validateReceiptChain(card) {
  1381	  const receipts = Array.isArray(card.receipts) ? card.receipts : [];
  1382	  let prevRecord = null;
  1383	  let chainStarted = false;
  1384	  for (const receipt of receipts) {
  1385	    if (
  1386	      receipt === null ||
  1387	      typeof receipt !== "object" ||
  1388	      Array.isArray(receipt)
  1389	    ) {
  1390	      fail("receipt chain entry must be an object");
  1391	    }
  1392	    if ("entryHash" in receipt) {
  1393	      if (receipt.entryHash === null) {
  1394	        fail("receipt chain entryHash must not be null");
  1395	      }
  1396	      if (!("prevEntryHash" in receipt)) {
  1397	        fail("receipt chain entry missing prevEntryHash");
  1398	      }
  1399	      const expectedPrev =
  1400	        prevRecord === null ? null : computeRecordEntryHash(prevRecord);
  1401	      if (receipt.prevEntryHash !== expectedPrev) {
  1402	        fail("receipt chain prevEntryHash mismatch");
  1403	      }
  1404	      const expected = computeRecordEntryHash(receipt);
  1405	      if (receipt.entryHash !== expected) {
  1406	        fail("receipt chain entryHash recompute mismatch");
  1407	      }
  1408	      prevRecord = receipt;
  1409	      chainStarted = true;
  1410	    } else {
  1411	      if (chainStarted) {
  1412	        fail("receipt chain broken by legacy record after chained record");
  1413	      }
  1414	      prevRecord = receipt;
  1415	    }
  1416	  }
  1417	}

  2667	    const tempMd = join(controlRoot, `WORKPLAN.md.tmp-${process.pid}`);
  2668	    await writeFile(tempMd, renderWorkplan(state));
  2669	    await rename(tempMd, workplanPath);
  2670	
  2671	    if (process.env.WORKPLAN_FAULT_AFTER_PROJECTION === "1") {
  2672	      fail("injected fault after projection");
  2673	    }
  2674	
  2675	    const tempState = join(
  2676	      controlRoot,
  2677	      ".workplan",
  2678	      `state.json.tmp-${process.pid}`,
  2679	    );
  2680	    await writeFile(tempState, `${JSON.stringify(state, null, 2)}\n`);
  2681	    await rename(tempState, statePath);
  2682	
  2683	    return { revision: state.revision, blocked: true };
  2684	  } finally {
  2685	    await releaseTransitionLock(acquired);
  2686	  }
  2687	}
  2688	
  2689	async function resume(options) {
  2690	  const { controlRoot, callerWorktree, expectedRevision, id, owner } = options;
  2691	
  2692	  if (!isAbsolute(controlRoot)) {
  2693	    fail("--control-root must be an absolute path");
  2694	  }
  2695	  if (!isAbsolute(callerWorktree)) {
  2696	    fail("--caller-worktree must be an absolute path");
  2697	  }
  2698	
  2699	  parseClaimOwner(owner);
  2700	  await assertSameRepository(controlRoot, callerWorktree);
  2701	
  2702	  const acquired = await acquireTransitionLock(controlRoot);
  2703	  try {
  2704	    const statePath = join(controlRoot, ".workplan", "state.json");
  2705	    const raw = await readFile(statePath, "utf8");
  2706	    const state = JSON.parse(raw);
  2707	    validateState(state);
  2708	
  2709	    if (state.revision !== expectedRevision) {
  2710	      fail(
  2711	        `expected revision ${expectedRevision} but found ${state.revision}`,
  2712	      );
  2713	    }
  2714	
  2715	    const card = state.cards.find((c) => c.id === id);
  2716	    if (!card) {
  2717	      fail(`card ${id} not found`);
  2718	    }
  2719	    if (card.status !== "blocked") {
  2720	      fail(`card ${card.id} is not in blocked status`);
  2721	    }
  2722	    if (card.owner !== owner) {
  2723	      fail(`card ${card.id} is not owned by ${owner}`);
  2724	    }
  2725	
  2726	    if (!card.blockedFrom || !BLOCKABLE_STATUSES.includes(card.blockedFrom)) {
  2727	      fail("invalid blockedFrom");
  2728	    }
  2729	    if (
  2730	      typeof card.blockReason !== "string" ||
  2731	      card.blockReason.trim().length === 0
  2732	    ) {
  2733	      fail("blockReason must be a non-empty string");
  2734	    }
  2735	    if (typeof card.retainPaths !== "boolean") {
  2736	      fail("retainPaths must be a boolean");
  2737	    }
  2738	
  2739	    const latestReceipt = card.receipts.at(-1);
  2740	    if (!latestReceipt || latestReceipt.to !== "blocked") {
  2741	      fail("latest receipt is not a blocked receipt");
  2742	    }
  2743	    validateBlockedReceipt(latestReceipt, card, expectedRevision);
  2744	
  2745	    if (["review", "verifying"].includes(card.blockedFrom)) {
  2746	      validateReviewFreeze(card);
  2747	    }
  2748	    if (card.blockedFrom === "verifying") {
  2749	      validateIntegrationReceipt(card, latestReceipt.revisionFrom);
  2750	    }
  2751	    validateReceiptChain(card);
  2752	
  2753	    validateTransitionCardBasics(card, "blocked");
  2754	    if (card.definition.worktreePath !== callerWorktree) {
  2755	      fail("worktreePath must match callerWorktree");
  2756	    }
  2757	    validateNoOwnerWip(owner, card, state);
  2758	    validateClaimPathExclusivity(card, state);
  2759	
  2760	    const blockedFrom = card.blockedFrom;
  2761	    const blockReason = card.blockReason;
  2762	    const retainPaths = card.retainPaths;
  2763	
  2764	    appendReceipt(
  2765	      card,
  2766	      "blocked",
  2767	      "in_progress",
  2768	      owner,
  2835	    const statePath = join(controlRoot, ".workplan", "state.json");
  2836	    const raw = await readFile(statePath, "utf8");
  2837	    const state = JSON.parse(raw);
  2838	    validateState(state);
  2839	
  2840	    if (state.revision !== expectedRevision) {
  2841	      fail(
  2842	        `expected revision ${expectedRevision} but found ${state.revision}`,
  2843	      );
  2844	    }
  2845	
  2846	    const card = state.cards.find((c) => c.id === id);
  2847	    if (!card) {
  2848	      fail(`card ${id} not found`);
  2849	    }
  2850	    if (card.status !== "backlog") {
  2851	      fail(`card ${id} is not in backlog status`);
  2852	    }
  2853	    if (!Array.isArray(card.paths) || card.paths.length === 0) {
  2854	      fail(`card ${id} must have nonempty paths`);
  2855	    }
  2856	    for (const path of card.paths) {
  2857	      validateOwnedPath(path);
  2858	    }

  2868	        }
  2869	      }
  2870	    }

  2873	    const expectedHash = computeDefinitionHash(definition);
  2874	    if (expectedHash !== definition.definitionHash) {
  2875	      fail("definitionHash mismatch");
  2876	    }
  2877	
  2878	    card.status = "ready";
  2879	    validatePathExclusivity(state);
  2880

  2882	    card.definition = definition;
  2883	    card.authorityGrant = { ...AUTHORITY_GRANT };
  2884	    appendReceipt(
  2885	      card,
  2886	      "backlog",
  2887	      "ready",
  2888	      owner,

    53	function execGit(args, options = {}) {
    54	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    55	    execFile("git", args, options, (error, stdout, stderr) => {
    56	      if (error) {
    57	        reject(
    58	          new Error(
    59	            `git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${stderr || error.message}`,
    60	          ),
    61	        );
    62	      } else {
    63	        resolve({ stdout, stderr });
    64	      }
    65	    });
    66	  });
    67	}

    69	async function repository(initialState, name = "primary") {
    70	  const parent = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "meta-harness-workplan-"));
    71	  const root = join(parent, name);
    72	  await mkdir(root, { recursive: true });
    73	  await execGit(["init"], { cwd: root });
    74	  await mkdir(join(root, ".workplan"));
    75	  await writeFile(
    76	    join(root, ".workplan", "state.json"),
    77	    `${JSON.stringify(initialState, null, 2)}\n`,
    78	  );
    79	  await writeFile(join(root, "WORKPLAN.md"), "# existing projection\n");
    80	  return { root, parent };
    81	}

   349	async function reachVerifying(
   350	  root,
   351	  id,
   352	  owner = null,
   353	  actor = null,
   354	) {
   355	  const actualOwner = owner || codexOwner("worker-1");
   356	  const actualActor = actor || coordinatorOwner("coordinator-1");
   357	  const { owner: returnedOwner, reviewHead } = await reachReview(root, id, actualOwner);
   358	  const integrationCommit = await mergeBranch(
   359	    root,
   360	    "review",
   361	    "integrate review",
   362	  );
   363	  const integrateResult = await run([
   364	    "integrate",
   365	    id,
   366	    "--control-root",
   367	    root,
   368	    "--caller-worktree",
   369	    root,
   370	    "--expected-revision",
   371	    "5",
   372	    "--actor",
   373	    actualActor,
   374	    "--integration-commit",
   375	    integrationCommit,
   376	  ]);
   377	  if (integrateResult.code !== 0) {
   378	    throw new Error(`integrate failed: ${integrateResult.stderr}`);
   379	  }
   380	  return { owner: returnedOwner, actor: actualActor, reviewHead, integrationCommit };
   381	}

  2311	function validateIntegrationReceiptBinding(card) {
  2312	  const receipts = Array.isArray(card.receipts) ? card.receipts : [];
  2313	  // A record is "chained" if the `entryHash` KEY is present (null or not);
  2314	  // validateReceiptChain rejects nulls upstream, but this binding check is
  2315	  // robust regardless so a future refactor that loosens the upstream
  2316	  // check cannot silently disable the binding enforcement here.
  2317	  const hasChainedRecord = receipts.some(
  2318	    (receipt) =>
  2319	      receipt !== null &&
  2320	      typeof receipt === "object" &&
  2321	      !Array.isArray(receipt) &&
  2322	      "entryHash" in receipt,
  2323	  );
  2324	  if (!hasChainedRecord) {
  2325	    return;
  2326	  }
  2327	  // Use the LAST to:"verifying" record as the binding anchor. A card that
  2328	  // re-integrates after block/resume appends a fresh verifying record; the
  2329	  // earlier one is a fossil that must NOT be used as the binding source, or
  2330	  // a replayed old integrationReceipt could be re-accepted against the old
  2331	  // record's binding hash.
  2332	  const verifyingRecords = receipts.filter(
  2333	    (receipt) => receipt.to === "verifying",
  2334	  );
  2335	  if (verifyingRecords.length === 0) {
  2336	    fail("integrationReceipt binding record missing in ledger");
  2337	  }
  2338	  const bindingRecord = verifyingRecords[verifyingRecords.length - 1];
  2339	  if (
  2340	    !bindingRecord.evidence ||
  2341	    bindingRecord.evidence.integrationReceiptHash === undefined ||
  2342	    bindingRecord.evidence.integrationReceiptHash === null
  2343	  ) {
  2344	    fail("integrationReceipt binding record missing in ledger");
  2345	  }
  2346	  const expectedHash = computeIntegrationReceiptHash(card.integrationReceipt);
  2347	  if (bindingRecord.evidence.integrationReceiptHash !== expectedHash) {
  2348	    fail("integrationReceipt binding hash mismatch");
  2349	  }
  2350	  // Positional rule: when the card is currently in `verifying`, the ledger
  2351	  // tail MUST be the same to:"verifying" record we just bound against. This
  2352	  // closes the multi-cycle tail-strip hole: deleting the latest verifying
  2353	  // record but replaying an older integrationReceipt that matches the old
  2354	  // binding hash would otherwise pass check (i) above. resume is unaffected
  2355	  // because a stripped middle record breaks prevEntryHash at the blocked
  2356	  // tail. block-from-verifying inherits this check via its own pre-mutation
  2357	  // validateReceiptChain + this binding call.
  2358	  if (card.status === "verifying") {
  2359	    const tail = receipts[receipts.length - 1];
  2360	    if (!tail || tail.to !== "verifying") {
  2361	      fail("ledger tail is not the verifying record");
  2362	    }
  2363	  }
  2364	}
